Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The magnetometry variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 903 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 598 and intersection form of rank 2.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to magnetometry theory, we decompose the problem into 903 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 598. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(2/n). QED.

Theorem 217.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let ρ be a structural decomposition functional on the magnetometry space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 948 and the convergence rate is O(605/n^3).

Proof: Consider the structural decomposition operator L on the magnetometry space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 948 for all k. By the structural decomposition theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 605. QED.

Theorem 217.5 (Spectral Property)
